- **daraja-auth**: Generates, caches and refreshes Safaricom Daraja OAuth 2.0 access tokens from the Consumer Key and Secret (client-credentials grant with HTTP Basic auth), including single-flight refresh, 401 retry and multi-instance caching. - **daraja-b2b**: Implements Safaricom Daraja B2B payments between shortcodes: BusinessPayBill (to a Paybill and account), BusinessBuyGoods (to a Till), BusinessPayToBulk (top up a B2C utility account) and B2B Express Checkout (a USSD push asking a merchant's till operator to pay your Paybill). - **daraja-c2b**: Implements Safaricom Daraja C2B (Customer-to-Business) for Paybill and Till numbers. - **daraja-callbacks**: Builds resilient Safaricom Daraja callback and webhook receivers: STK CallBackURL; C2B Validation and Confirmation; ResultURL and QueueTimeOutURL for B2C, B2B, tax, status, reversal and balance; Ratiba, Bill Manager and B2B Express callbacks. - **daraja-checkout**: End-to-end recipe for adding M-Pesa checkout to an existing web or mobile app with STK Push. - **daraja-credentials-security**: Manages Safaricom Daraja secrets safely (Consumer Key and Secret, M-Pesa Express passkey, initiator password, SecurityCredential) and generates the SecurityCredential by RSA-encrypting the initiator password with Safaricom's sandbox or production certificate. - **daraja-dynamic-qr**: Generates M-Pesa Dynamic QR codes with Safaricom Daraja for Buy Goods, Paybill, agent withdrawal, send money or send-to-business, with a preset amount and reference, and renders the returned base64 PNG. - **darbar-handoff**: Run BEFORE compacting or ending a session so nothing that exists only in the conversation is lost. Sweeps for unfiled work, decisions and their reasoning, gotchas and blocked items, routes each to its ONE home (task row, note, charter, agent-instruction file, memory), and writes the snapshot the SessionStart hook prints back. Records, unanswered questions and things already visible elsewhere are not rows; on boards colleagues read it proposes instead of filing. Use before /compact or /clear, when wrapping up or handing off, or when asked if everything is in sync with the board. ([RajVarsani/skills](https://github.com/RajVarsani/skills/tree/HEAD/darbar-handoff))
- **darbar-project-init**: Bootstrap a Darbar-tracked project and then run it as a strict orchestrate-only manager. Day-zero or retro-init of work already underway. Also THE operating manual the other darbar skills defer to - every task, review-lane, feedback, project and agent lifecycle as the engine really implements it, plus the orchestration and token-economics doctrine for whichever model and harness is reading it. Use for "init project", "new darbar project", "put this under darbar", or taking over an existing… ([RajVarsani/skills](https://github.com/RajVarsani/skills/tree/HEAD/darbar-project-init))
- **darbar-resume**: Rehydrate a Darbar orchestrator after a compaction, clear, resume, or when a fresh session inherits a live board. Read half of the pair whose write half is darbar-handoff: re-reads state from the API (board, charter, event log, processes) and re-loads the doctrine from darbar-project-init, the authority it deliberately does not restate. State survives a compaction; the operating manual does not. Use after compacting, when taking over a board mid-flight, or before dispatching when unsure you still know how this project is run.
